TRADER TRADE LOG - T1 STRATEGY - TOP 5 STOCKS PER SESSION
=========================================================================================================

Sizing: fixed capital per entry = 10,000; shares = nearest whole number (1 share if price > capital).
Money Invested/Exited: Shares*EntryPrice on ENTRY, Shares*ExitPrice on EXIT.
Total Money Invested: running active exposure (entries add, exits release cost).
Total Profit/Loss: running realised P&L; updates on EXIT rows only.
Gain/Loss % and Gain/Loss (Absolute) are populated only on EXIT rows.
Days Taken counts trading sessions, not calendar days.
OPEN means the trade has no resolved exit yet.
A trade with several ENTRY rows (same Trade ID) was added to by later, higher-scoring signals (~10,000 each); its single EXIT sells all of it.
Gain/Loss % on an EXIT row is over the total invested in that trade; Days Taken counts from its first entry.
Status trailing_stop_hit = exited by the 30.0% trailing stop.
